                text "This list includes"
                anchor [ref=3383, url=https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sovereign_state]
                  text "sovereign states"
                text ", inhabited"
                anchor [ref=3384, loc=href:/wiki/Dependent_territory, url=https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dependent_territory]
                  text "dependent territories"
                text "and, in some cases, constituent countries of sovereign states, with inclusion within the list being primarily based on the"
                anchor [ref=3385, loc=href:/wiki/International_Organization_for_Standardization, url=https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_Organization_for_Standardization]
                  text "ISO"
                text "standard"
                anchor [ref=3386, loc=href:/wiki/ISO_3166-1, url=https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISO_3166-1]
                  text "ISO 3166-1"
                text ". For instance, the"
                anchor [ref=3387, loc=href:/wiki/United_Kingdom, url=https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Kingdom]
                  text "United Kingdom"
                text "is considered a single entity, while the constituent countries of the"
                anchor [ref=3388, url=https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kingdom_of_the_Netherlands]
                  text "Kingdom of the Netherlands"
                text "are considered separately. In addition, this list includes certain"
                anchor [ref=3389, url=https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_states_with_limited_recognition]
                  text "states with limited recognition"
                text "not found in ISO 3166-1. Also given in a percentage is each country's population compared with the"
                anchor [ref=3390, url=https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_population]
                  text "world population"
                text ", which the"
                anchor [ref=3391, url=https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations]
                  text "United Nations"
                text "estimated at 8.232 billion as of 2025."

                  text "1"
                  text "]"
              paragraph
                text "Figures used in this chart are based on the most up-to-date estimates or"
                anchor [ref=3393, loc=href:/wiki/Population_projection, url=https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Population_projection]
                  text "projections"
                text "by the national"
                anchor [ref=3401, loc=href:/wiki/Census, url=https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Census]
                  text "census"
                text "authority, where available, and are usually rounded off. Where updated national data are not available, figures are based on the estimates or projections for 2024 by the Population Division of the"
                anchor [ref=3402, url=https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Department_of_Economic_and_Social_Affairs]
                  text "United Nations Department of Economic and Social Affairs"
